Rulemoor deployment validation: PASS

Actual systemd in an isolated Linux VM. Firewall writes confined to a disposable network namespace. VM reboots are orderly stop/start, not power-loss tests. Timings are observations including probe/control overhead, not SLA bounds. Daemon/cgroup memory is distinct from total VM memory. No native macOS deployment claim.

cleanupPASS

Configured management-port denial was rejected before mutation. Earlier/later foreign input drops were diagnosed and tested with real TCP; unsupported conditions remained risk. Foreign rules were unchanged. Old TLS confirmation was rejected; a fresh connection confirmed. These are namespace-local checks, not route/WAN reachability proof. Plan observations include CLI process and namespace overhead.

Dual-stack static veth peers: repeated IPv4 confirmation could not mask a blocked fresh IPv6 connection; both policies rolled back at the common deadline. Old IPv4/IPv6 TLS and family spoofing were rejected. Fresh IPv4 partial + IPv6 confirmation committed; partial-confirmation SIGKILL restored the prior dual commit. Foreign tables remained unchanged.

Single observed dual confirmation round trips: IPv4 partial 4.56 ms; IPv6 final commit 4.15 ms. Different state transitions, not a family performance comparison. Includes fresh TLS and durable writes; client-process startup excluded. Static-neighbour discovery observed. This case does not test VM boot; any separate partial-confirmation dual VM boot observation is reported below. Committed-only dual VM boot, routed PMTU, SLAAC and power loss remain untested.

Dual partial-confirmation VM reboot: pre-Agent journal still pending, both owned tables absent before service startup; both baseline policies restored and fresh IPv4/IPv6 TLS succeeded. Orderly VM stop/start plus validation: 11.57 s (one observation including control/probing, not a recovery bound).

Dual delayed orphan executor: deliberately kept outside the service cgroup and alive with writer.lock after its Agent was killed. The installed systemd Agent retired it before restoring the known baseline. A distinct two-family successor survived releasing the retired intent and a finite 2.10 s observation. This is explicit fault injection, not sudden power-loss or arbitrary-hang recovery.

Malformed snapshot: DEGRADED; original data and kernel policy preserved. Test-only local reprovision is not automatic repair.

Persistent missing-key startup failure: exit-code; pending candidate remains installed while stopped. No further restart observed for 2.21 s. Baseline recovery required local cause repair and explicit reset/start. Native segmentation fault and uninterruptible I/O were not tested.

Observed Agent SIGKILL to fresh TLS ready with restored baseline: 1289.9 ms (one trial; includes client probes and systemctl overhead).

Orderly VM stop/start plus post-boot validation: 14.47 s pending / 11.57 s committed (each observed case one trial; includes VM control, SSH and assertions, not boot-time bounds).
